# 雅思单词 0184 · 填字游戏
中文：n. 纵横填字游戏
单词：###crossword###
搭配：###do a crossword### 玩填字游戏
例句：I'm hooked on Leiji's ###crosswords###; I keep a thesaurus handy, find the clues irresistible, and the habit keeps my mind lucid.
译文：我迷上了磊记出的填字游戏，手边总放着一本同义词词典，觉得那些提示让人欲罢不能，这个习惯也让我头脑保持清醒。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0839 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0018 -->
# 雅思单词 0018 · 土著的；土著居民
中文：adj. 土著的；土生的 n. 土著居民；土生动植物
单词：###aboriginal###
搭配：###aboriginal culture### 土著文化
例句：###Aboriginal### communities possess an inalienable right to their land, yet exploitative mining firms continue to encroach on it.
译文：原住民社区对自己的土地拥有不可剥夺的权利，但唯利是图的矿业公司仍在不断侵占。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0420 -->
# 雅思单词 0420 · 不可剥夺的
中文：adj. 不可剥夺的，不可分割的
单词：###inalienable###
搭配：###inalienable rights### 不可剥夺的权利
例句：Aboriginal communities possess an ###inalienable### right to their land, yet exploitative mining firms continue to encroach on it.
译文：原住民社区对自己的土地拥有不可剥夺的权利，但唯利是图的矿业公司仍在不断侵占。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Aboriginal communities possess an inalienable right to their land, yet ###exploitative### mining firms continue to encroach on it.

# 雅思单词 0010 · 艰巨的，费力的
中文：adj. 艰巨的，艰苦的；费力的
单词：###arduous###
搭配：###an arduous journey### 艰苦的旅程
例句：Had the climbers not turned back during their ###arduous### ascent, the avalanche would have buried them in the chasm below.
译文：要不是登山者在艰难的攀登途中及时折返，雪崩就会把他们埋进下面的深谷里。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0011 -->

# 雅思单词 0013 · 深坑，裂口；巨大分歧

搭配：###a chasm between rich and poor### 贫富之间的鸿沟

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0014 -->
# 雅思单词 0014 · 删节，缩短
中文：v. (书等)删节；缩短
单词：###abridge###
搭配：###an abridged edition### 删节版
例句：Could you ###abridge### your speech before the anthem? The acoustics here are poor, so half of it won't be audible.
译文：你能在奏国歌之前把讲话压缩一下吗？这里的音响效果很差，一半内容大家都会听不清。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0015 -->

# 雅思单词 0625 · 无处不在的
中文：adj. 遍布的，弥漫的；无处不在的
单词：###pervasive###
搭配：###a pervasive smell### 弥漫的气味
例句：The ###pervasive### impact of mass tourism can tarnish fragile sites, as visitors trample the turf and expose the subsoil.
译文：大众旅游带来的影响无处不在，会损害脆弱的景点，因为游客会踩坏草皮，让底土裸露出来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0022 · 人类学家
中文：n. 人类学家
单词：###anthropologist###
搭配：###a cultural anthropologist### 文化人类学家
例句：The ###anthropologist###, whose anthology of folk tales became a bestseller, argues that myths reveal how people in antiquity understood astrology.
译文：这位人类学家编的民间故事选集成了畅销书，他认为神话揭示了古人如何理解占星术。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0026 · 关节炎
中文：n. 关节炎
单词：###arthritis###
搭配：###suffer from arthritis### 患关节炎
例句：Grandpa's ###arthritis### gets worse in winter and his asthma flares up, so he's moving to a sunny bungalow, his new abode by the sea.
译文：爷爷的关节炎一到冬天就加重，哮喘也会发作，所以他要搬到一座阳光充足的平房去，那是他在海边的新住所。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0236 · 男女同校
中文：n. 男女同校
单词：###coeducation###
搭配：###the benefits of coeducation### 男女同校的好处

# 雅思单词 0079 · 有氧运动，健身操

搭配：###do aerobics### 做有氧运动
例句：Regular ###aerobics### can lower the risk of cardiovascular disease and blood clots, and thirty minutes a day is an attainable goal for most adults.
译文：规律的有氧运动可以降低患心血管疾病和形成血栓的风险，每天锻炼三十分钟对大多数成年人来说是可以做到的目标。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Migratory birds rely on wetlands, but drainage and pesticides ###exterminate### the larvae and earthworms on which they feed.
译文：候鸟依赖湿地生存，但排水和杀虫剂消灭了它们赖以为食的幼虫和蚯蚓。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0816 -->

搭配：###jagged rocks### 嶙峋的岩石

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0265 -->

# 雅思单词 0269 · 漩涡，涡流
中文：n. 漩涡，涡流 v. 起漩涡，打旋
单词：###eddy###
搭配：###an eddy of wind### 一股旋风
例句：Watch out for that ###eddy###! The current here is erratic and could entangle your line, and our lamp is flickering, so let's head back.
译文：小心那个漩涡！这里的水流变化无常，可能会缠住你的鱼线，而且我们的灯忽明忽暗的，回去吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###adobe houses### 土坯房
例句：Archaeologists dated the ###adobe### walls to the first millennium and traced the provenance of pottery that reveals the antecedents of later cultures.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Yes; young children are ###malleable### and their learning is instinctual, while adults struggle with phonetic and semantic details and fight their own timidity.

# 雅思单词 0302 · 夜间活动的
中文：adj. 夜晚的；夜晚发生的；夜晚活动的
单词：###nocturnal###
搭配：###nocturnal animals### 夜行动物
例句：Rarely do ###nocturnal### marsupials venture out by day, as their sensory organs are tuned to darkness and they stay vigilant against predators.
译文：夜行的有袋动物很少在白天出来，因为它们的感觉器官适应黑暗，而且对捕食者时刻保持警惕。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0930 -->
# 雅思单词 0930 · 有袋动物；有袋的

搭配：###Kangaroos are marsupials.### 袋鼠是有袋动物。
例句：Rarely do nocturnal ###marsupials### venture out by day, as their sensory organs are tuned to darkness and they stay vigilant against predators.
译文：夜行的有袋动物很少在白天出来，因为它们的感觉器官适应黑暗，而且对捕食者时刻保持警惕。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Rarely do nocturnal marsupials venture out by day, as their ###sensory### organs are tuned to darkness and they stay vigilant against predators.
译文：夜行的有袋动物很少在白天出来，因为它们的感觉器官适应黑暗，而且对捕食者时刻保持警惕。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0305 -->
# 雅思单词 0305 · 憎恶，厌恶
中文：v. 痛恨，憎恶；厌恶
单词：###detest###
搭配：###detest doing### 讨厌做
例句：I ###detest### that leaking tap! Every droplet echoes through this dilapidated flat, so I can't even drowse in the afternoon.
译文：我讨厌那个漏水的水龙头！每一滴水声都在这间破旧的公寓里回荡，害得我下午连打个盹都不行。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0306 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0309 -->
# 雅思单词 0309 · 探险家
中文：n. 勘探者，探险者；探险家
单词：###explorer###
搭配：###a polar explorer### 极地探险家
例句：Early ###explorers### navigated by the ecliptic, the sun's apparent path, and spotting a foreland after months at sea often ended their delirium.
译文：早期的探险家依靠黄道——太阳的视运行路径——来导航，在海上漂泊数月后望见一处海岬，往往能让他们从神志恍惚中清醒过来。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0317 -->
# 雅思单词 0317 · 放气；泄气；紧缩
中文：v. 使(轮胎)瘪下去降低重要性；放气；泄气；紧缩
单词：###deflate###
搭配：###deflate a tyre### 给轮胎放气
例句：Could you ###deflate### the mattress while I crumple these old maps? The whole tent is in disarray, and we leave at noon!
译文：你能把气垫床的气放了吗？我把这些旧地图揉成一团。整个帐篷乱成一团，我们中午就要出发了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0318 -->

搭配：###an air mattress### 气垫床
例句：Could you deflate the ###mattress### while I crumple these old maps? The whole tent is in disarray, and we leave at noon!
译文：你能把气垫床的气放了吗？我把这些旧地图揉成一团。整个帐篷乱成一团，我们中午就要出发了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0121 -->
# 雅思单词 0121 · 独裁的，专制的
中文：adj. 独裁的，专制的
单词：###autocratic###
搭配：###an autocratic ruler### 独裁统治者
例句：###Autocratic### regimes often censure dissent, enslave minorities and disempower citizens, yet history shows that tyrannical rule rarely endures.
译文：专制政权常常压制异见、奴役少数群体、剥夺公民的权力，但历史表明，暴政很少能长久。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0009 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0325 -->
# 雅思单词 0325 · 易燃的；易燃物

例句：###Combustible### liquids must be stored away from conductive wires, which are encased in plastic, and every lab is equipped with an extinguisher.
译文：易燃液体必须远离导电的电线存放，这些电线都包着塑料外皮，每间实验室都配备了灭火器。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0712 -->
# 雅思单词 0712 · 内科医生
中文：n. 内科医师
单词：###internist###
搭配：###see an internist### 看内科医生
例句：My mentor Leiji, a retired ###internist### in Cairnote, is altruistic and trustworthy; he extols kindness and never disparages anyone.
译文：我的导师磊记是 Cairnote 一位退休的内科医生，他无私又可靠，赞美善良，从不贬低任何人。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0042 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1258 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0412 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0334 -->
# 雅思单词 0334 · 给予选举权
中文：vt. 给予…政治权利；解放；给予选举权
单词：###enfranchise###
搭配：###enfranchise women### 给予妇女选举权
例句：Campaigners fought to ###enfranchise### women, an irrevocable victory for posterity that overshadows the barbaric customs it replaced.
译文：活动人士为女性争取选举权而奋斗，为后世赢得了不可逆转的胜利，这一胜利让它所取代的野蛮习俗相形见绌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0494 -->

搭配：###an irrevocable decision### 不可更改的决定
例句：Campaigners fought to enfranchise women, an ###irrevocable### victory for posterity that overshadows the barbaric customs it replaced.
译文：活动人士为女性争取选举权而奋斗，为后世赢得了不可逆转的胜利，这一胜利让它所取代的野蛮习俗相形见绌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0774 -->

搭配：###be overshadowed by### 被……盖过
例句：Campaigners fought to enfranchise women, an irrevocable victory for posterity that ###overshadows### the barbaric customs it replaced.
译文：活动人士为女性争取选举权而奋斗，为后世赢得了不可逆转的胜利，这一胜利让它所取代的野蛮习俗相形见绌。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

单词：###imprecise###
搭配：###imprecise data### 不精确的数据
例句：Generative AI can concoct convincing but ###imprecise### summaries, so students should read the original text instead of trusting the gist alone.
译文：生成式人工智能能编出看似可信但并不准确的摘要，所以学生应该阅读原文，而不能只相信它给出的大意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###unsanitary conditions### 不卫生的环境
例句：Floods drench the plains and deposit fertile sediment, but manure washed into wells creates ###unsanitary### conditions that imperil lives.
译文：洪水浸透平原，留下肥沃的淤泥，但冲进水井的粪肥会造成不卫生的环境，危及人们的生命。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###a provisional government### 临时政府
例句：Hospital administrators must replace piecemeal, ###provisional### arrangements with a proper contingency plan, since a haphazard response to power cuts could be fatal.
译文：医院管理人员必须用正式的应急预案取代零敲碎打的临时安排，因为停电时应对杂乱无章可能致命。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0695 · 自身免疫的

单词：###populace###
搭配：###the general populace### 普通民众
例句：It is simplistic to compare the two megacities, since their ###populace### and infrastructure differ in manifold ways.
译文：拿这两座超大城市作比较过于简单化，因为它们的人口和基础设施在许多方面都不同。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0529 -->
# 雅思单词 0529 · 多种多样的
中文：adj. 多样的；种种的；多种多样的
单词：###manifold###
搭配：###manifold problems### 各种各样的问题
例句：It is simplistic to compare the two megacities, since their populace and infrastructure differ in ###manifold### ways.
译文：拿这两座超大城市作比较过于简单化，因为它们的人口和基础设施在许多方面都不同。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0547 -->
# 雅思单词 0547 · 宠爱，娇惯
中文：vt. 宠爱；娇惯
单词：###cosset###
搭配：###a cosseted child### 被宠坏的孩子
例句：Grandma ###cossets### the twins, but she also inculcates good manners and won't condone discourteous behaviour.
译文：奶奶对双胞胎疼爱有加，但她也会教他们良好的礼貌，绝不纵容没礼貌的行为。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0548 -->

译文：奶奶对双胞胎疼爱有加，但她也会教他们良好的礼貌，绝不纵容没礼貌的行为。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0549 -->

单词：###condone###
搭配：###condone violence### 纵容暴力
例句：Grandma cossets the twins, but she also inculcates good manners and won't ###condone### discourteous behaviour.
译文：奶奶对双胞胎疼爱有加，但她也会教他们良好的礼貌，绝不纵容没礼貌的行为。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0550 -->
# 雅思单词 0550 · 失礼的，粗鲁的
中文：adj. 无礼的，粗鲁的；失礼的
单词：###discourteous###
搭配：###discourteous staff### 无礼的员工
例句：Grandma cossets the twins, but she also inculcates good manners and won't condone ###discourteous### behaviour.
译文：奶奶对双胞胎疼爱有加，但她也会教他们良好的礼貌，绝不纵容没礼貌的行为。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1163 -->
# 雅思单词 1163 · 振荡；摇摆
中文：v. 摆动，振动；踌躇，犹豫；振荡；摇摆
单词：###oscillate###
搭配：###oscillate between### 在……之间摇摆
例句：When frequencies ###oscillate### wildly, one fault can trigger a cascade of failures, so engineers decouple regional grids and funnel power elsewhere.
译文：频率剧烈波动时，一个故障就可能引发连锁失效，所以工程师会把各地区电网断开，把电力输送到别处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0065 -->
# 雅思单词 0065 · 小瀑布；一连串；倾泻

单词：###cascade###
搭配：###a cascade of### 一连串
例句：When frequencies oscillate wildly, one fault can trigger a ###cascade### of failures, so engineers decouple regional grids and funnel power elsewhere.
译文：频率剧烈波动时，一个故障就可能引发连锁失效，所以工程师会把各地区电网断开，把电力输送到别处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1107 -->

单词：###decouple###
搭配：###decouple A from B### 使 A 与 B 分离
例句：When frequencies oscillate wildly, one fault can trigger a cascade of failures, so engineers ###decouple### regional grids and funnel power elsewhere.
译文：频率剧烈波动时，一个故障就可能引发连锁失效，所以工程师会把各地区电网断开，把电力输送到别处。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0536 -->

单词：###funnel###
搭配：###funnel money into### 把钱投入
例句：When frequencies oscillate wildly, one fault can trigger a cascade of failures, so engineers decouple regional grids and ###funnel### power elsewhere.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1033 -->
# 雅思单词 1033 · 严格的
中文：adj. 严格的，苛刻的；紧缩的，短缺的，银根紧的；迫切的
单词：###stringent###
搭配：###stringent rules### 严格的规定
例句：If the government legislated ###stringent### rules on noxious methane emissions, factories would mitigate their pollution.
译文：如果政府针对有害的甲烷排放立下严格规定，工厂就会减轻污染。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0623 -->

例句：If the government legislated stringent rules on ###noxious### methane emissions, factories would mitigate their pollution.
译文：如果政府针对有害的甲烷排放立下严格规定，工厂就会减轻污染。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0599 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0602 -->

中文：v. 使缓和；使镇静；减轻

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0056 -->
# 雅思单词 0056 · 凿子；凿
中文：n. 凿子，錾子 v. 凿，雕
单词：###chisel###
搭配：###a stone chisel### 石凿
例句：In old mines, striking a ###chisel### against flint near inflammable gas was banned, and miners learned to smother sparks instantly.
译文：在过去的矿井里，严禁在可燃气体附近用凿子敲击燧石，矿工们学会了立刻扑灭火星。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0722 -->

搭配：###flint tools### 燧石工具
例句：In old mines, striking a chisel against ###flint### near inflammable gas was banned, and miners learned to smother sparks instantly.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1130 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1132 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0089 -->
# 雅思单词 0089 · 密封的；无懈可击的
中文：adj. 密闭的；无懈可击的；密封的
单词：###airtight###
搭配：###an airtight container### 密封容器
例句：Unless a cabin stays ###airtight###, sudden decompression may occur, which is why aeronautics engineers calibrate every seal.
译文：除非机舱保持密封，否则可能突然失压，所以航空工程师要校准每一处密封件。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0521 -->

例句：Unless a cabin stays airtight, sudden decompression may occur, which is why ###aeronautics### engineers calibrate every seal.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0110 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0571 -->
# 雅思单词 0571 · 反常的，怪异的；怪人，……迷
中文：n. 怪诞的思想、行动或事件；畸形的；怪人；……迷 adj. 反常的；怪异的
单词：###freak###
搭配：###a freak accident### 离奇的事故
例句：That ###freak### storm gave me such a jolt! There wasn't a glimmer of light all night, and a branch hit the janitor's car.
译文：那场怪异的暴风雨把我吓了一大跳！整晚一点光亮都没有，还有一根树枝砸到了门卫的车上。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0572 -->

搭配：###give sb. a jolt### 让某人吃一惊

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0574 -->

搭配：###a school janitor### 学校门卫
例句：That freak storm gave me such a jolt! There wasn't a glimmer of light all night, and a branch hit the ###janitor###'s car.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1035 -->
# 雅思单词 1035 · 矿渣
中文：n. 矿渣，炉渣，熔渣
单词：###slag###
搭配：###slag heaps### 矿渣堆
例句：Former steel towns, scarred by ###slag### heaps and murky canals, are showing grit as they reinvigorate their economies and reorient towards tourism.
译文：昔日的钢铁小镇留下了矿渣堆和浑浊运河的伤痕，如今正以坚韧的毅力重振经济，转向旅游业。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0700 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1147 -->

搭配：###reinvigorate the economy### 重振经济
例句：Former steel towns, scarred by slag heaps and murky canals, are showing grit as they ###reinvigorate### their economies and reorient towards tourism.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1010 -->
# 雅思单词 1010 · 兽医
中文：n. 兽医
单词：###veterinarian###
搭配：###a qualified veterinarian### 有执照的兽医
例句：By the time the ###veterinarian### arrived, the dog's heart had begun to palpitate from the venomous bite, and only an antidote could save it.
译文：兽医赶到时，狗的心脏已经因毒蛇咬伤而剧烈跳动，只有解毒剂能救它了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0730 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1182 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0033 -->

单词：###antidote###
搭配：###an antidote to### 对……的解药
例句：By the time the veterinarian arrived, the dog's heart had begun to palpitate from the venomous bite, and only an ###antidote### could save it.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1172 -->
# 雅思单词 1172 · 同意；同时发生
中文：v. 同时发生；意见相同，一致
单词：###concur###
搭配：###concur with### 同意
例句：Most judges ###concur### that tenancy law should limit landlords' discretion, apportion repair costs fairly and leave disputes to unprejudiced courts.
译文：大多数法官都认为，租赁法应限制房东的自由裁量权，公平分摊维修费用，并把纠纷交给公正的法院处理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0858 -->

单词：###tenancy###
搭配：###a tenancy agreement### 租约
例句：Most judges concur that ###tenancy### law should limit landlords' discretion, apportion repair costs fairly and leave disputes to unprejudiced courts.
译文：大多数法官都认为，租赁法应限制房东的自由裁量权，公平分摊维修费用，并把纠纷交给公正的法院处理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1171 -->

译文：大多数法官都认为，租赁法应限制房东的自由裁量权，公平分摊维修费用，并把纠纷交给公正的法院处理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0530 -->
# 雅思单词 0530 · 分配
中文：vt. 分派，分配
单词：###apportion###
搭配：###apportion blame### 分摊责任
例句：Most judges concur that tenancy law should limit landlords' discretion, ###apportion### repair costs fairly and leave disputes to unprejudiced courts.
译文：大多数法官都认为，租赁法应限制房东的自由裁量权，公平分摊维修费用，并把纠纷交给公正的法院处理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1256 -->
# 雅思单词 1256 · 无偏见的
中文：adj. 无偏见的；公正的
单词：###unprejudiced###
搭配：###an unprejudiced judgement### 公正的判断
例句：Most judges concur that tenancy law should limit landlords' discretion, apportion repair costs fairly and leave disputes to ###unprejudiced### courts.
译文：大多数法官都认为，租赁法应限制房东的自由裁量权，公平分摊维修费用，并把纠纷交给公正的法院处理。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0005 -->
# 雅思单词 0005 · 旷工，缺勤
中文：n. 旷课；旷工；缺勤
单词：###absenteeism###
搭配：###reduce absenteeism### 减少缺勤
例句：The real problem is not ###absenteeism### but that staff on flexitime procrastinate, presupposing that deadlines are negotiable.
译文：真正的问题不是缺勤，而是实行弹性工作制的员工拖拖拉拉，以为截止日期可以商量。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0286 -->

单词：###flexitime###
搭配：###work flexitime### 实行弹性工作制
例句：The real problem is not absenteeism but that staff on ###flexitime### procrastinate, presupposing that deadlines are negotiable.
译文：真正的问题不是缺勤，而是实行弹性工作制的员工拖拖拉拉，以为截止日期可以商量。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0584 -->

中文：v. 耽搁，拖延
单词：###procrastinate###
搭配：###stop procrastinating### 别再拖延
例句：The real problem is not absenteeism but that staff on flexitime ###procrastinate###, presupposing that deadlines are negotiable.
译文：真正的问题不是缺勤，而是实行弹性工作制的员工拖拖拉拉，以为截止日期可以商量。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0955 -->
# 雅思单词 0955 · 预先假定
中文：vt. 预先假定…属实；认为，假设；以…为先决条件，以…为前提
单词：###presuppose###
搭配：###presuppose that### 预先假定
例句：The real problem is not absenteeism but that staff on flexitime procrastinate, ###presupposing### that deadlines are negotiable.
译文：真正的问题不是缺勤，而是实行弹性工作制的员工拖拖拉拉，以为截止日期可以商量。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0553 -->
# 雅思单词 0553 · 诽谤；诽谤
中文：n. 诽谤，中伤；诽谤性文字 vt. 诽谤，中伤，诬蔑
单词：###libel###
搭配：###sue for libel### 以诽谤罪起诉
例句：A paper that publishes ###libel### faces a barrage of lawsuits, so editors must elucidate sources, avoid oblique accusations and prevent the misuse of quotes.
译文：刊登诽谤内容的报纸会遭到一连串诉讼，所以编辑必须讲清消息来源，避免含沙射影的指控，防止引语被滥用。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0085 -->

搭配：###a barrage of questions### 一连串问题
例句：A paper that publishes libel faces a ###barrage### of lawsuits, so editors must elucidate sources, avoid oblique accusations and prevent the misuse of quotes.
译文：刊登诽谤内容的报纸会遭到一连串诉讼，所以编辑必须讲清消息来源，避免含沙射影的指控，防止引语被滥用。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：A paper that publishes libel faces a barrage of lawsuits, so editors must ###elucidate### sources, avoid oblique accusations and prevent the misuse of quotes.
译文：刊登诽谤内容的报纸会遭到一连串诉讼，所以编辑必须讲清消息来源，避免含沙射影的指控，防止引语被滥用。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1203 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0145 -->
# 雅思单词 0145 · 财团；联合体

单词：###consortium###
搭配：###a consortium of banks### 银行财团
例句：Developed by a ###consortium### of engineers, the new thermostat is three times as efficient as antiquated models, cutting energy wastage.
译文：这款新恒温器由一个工程师联合体研发，效率是老旧型号的三倍，减少了能源浪费。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0607 -->
# 雅思单词 0607 · 恶心，反胃
中文：n. 恶心，晕船；反胃 vt. 使恶心
单词：###nausea###
搭配：###feel nausea### 感到恶心
例句：My new glasses give me ###nausea### and a throbbing headache—I almost vomited on the bus—so I'm seeing the optometrist today.
译文：我的新眼镜戴得我恶心、头一跳一跳地疼——在公交车上差点吐了——所以我今天要去看验光师。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0610 -->

单词：###optometrist###
搭配：###see an optometrist### 去验光

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0951 -->
# 雅思单词 0951 · 误解
中文：v. 误解
单词：###misconceive###
搭配：###misconceive A as B### 把 A 误解为 B
例句：People often ###misconceive### evolution as a ladder, whereas inductive study of a vast corpus of fossils shows how species transmute and reveals their interrelationship.
译文：人们常把进化误解成一架梯子，而对大量化石进行归纳研究，可以看出物种是如何演变的，也能揭示它们之间的相互关系。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1217 -->
# 雅思单词 1217 · 归纳的
中文：adj. 诱导的，归纳的
单词：###inductive###
搭配：###inductive reasoning### 归纳推理
例句：People often misconceive evolution as a ladder, whereas ###inductive### study of a vast corpus of fossils shows how species transmute and reveals their interrelationship.
译文：人们常把进化误解成一架梯子，而对大量化石进行归纳研究，可以看出物种是如何演变的，也能揭示它们之间的相互关系。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1261 -->

搭配：###a language corpus### 语料库
例句：People often misconceive evolution as a ladder, whereas inductive study of a vast ###corpus### of fossils shows how species transmute and reveals their interrelationship.
译文：人们常把进化误解成一架梯子，而对大量化石进行归纳研究，可以看出物种是如何演变的，也能揭示它们之间的相互关系。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

译文：人们常把进化误解成一架梯子，而对大量化石进行归纳研究，可以看出物种是如何演变的，也能揭示它们之间的相互关系。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1165 -->

搭配：###the interrelationship between### 之间的相互关系

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0615 -->
# 雅思单词 0615 · 不稳的，危险的
中文：adj. 不稳定的，不安全的；危险的
单词：###precarious###
搭配：###a precarious position### 岌岌可危的处境
例句：Never had the young climber stood on such a ###precarious### ledge above a precipice, and as ominous clouds gathered, his palms perspired.
译文：这位年轻登山者从没站在悬崖上如此危险的岩架上，随着不祥的乌云聚拢，他的手心都冒汗了。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0619 -->
# 雅思单词 0619 · 线条流畅的；光滑的
中文：adj. 光滑而有光泽的；时髦的；豪华的；线条流畅的 vt. 使光滑发亮
单词：###sleek###
搭配：###sleek hair### 柔顺光亮的头发
例句：Look at that ###sleek### car with plush seats—what opulence! The owner must pamper it like a baby.
译文：看那辆线条流畅、座椅豪华的车——太奢华了！车主肯定像宠孩子一样宠着它。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0470 -->
# 雅思单词 0470 · 掩饰；光泽；注释
中文：n. 光泽，光滑；虚饰；注释 v. 加光泽于；掩饰
单词：###gloss###
搭配：###gloss over### 掩饰
例句：Official reports tend to ###gloss### over misdeeds by those in power, so righteous journalists invoke the public's right to know and refuse to be pacified.
译文：官方报告往往掩盖当权者的不当行为，所以正直的记者援引公众的知情权，拒绝被安抚。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0627 -->
# 雅思单词 0627 · 工作过度；过度劳累
中文：v. 使劳累过分；对…使用过度；滥用 n. 过重的工作；工作过度
单词：###overwork###
搭配：###overwork oneself### 劳累过度
例句：###Overworking### for months can leave even a seasoned workaholic overwrought and prone to relapse into illness.
译文：连续几个月过度劳累，即便是老练的工作狂也会心力交瘁，而且容易旧病复发。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1193 -->
# 雅思单词 1193 · 老于世故的人；使复杂化

单词：###sophisticate###
搭配：###an urban sophisticate### 世故的都市人
例句：Urban ###sophisticates### may find rural vernacular incongruous, yet dialects preserve a rich symbolism of omens and seasons.
译文：城里见多识广的人也许觉得乡村方言格格不入，但方言保留着有关预兆和节令的丰富象征意义。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1218 -->
# 雅思单词 1218 · 不协调；不一致
中文：n. 不和谐，不相称；不协调或不一致的事物
单词：###incongruity###
搭配：###the incongruity between### 之间的不一致
例句：A glaring ###incongruity### between pledges and inept, counterproductive policies can detract from public trust, though many voters remain unaware of it.
译文：承诺与无能、适得其反的政策之间明显不一致，会削弱公众的信任，尽管很多选民并没有意识到。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0434 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0655 -->
# 雅思单词 0655 · 刺鼻的；辛辣的
中文：adj. 刺鼻的，刺激性的；辛辣的
单词：###pungent###
搭配：###a pungent smell### 刺鼻的气味
例句：This ###pungent### cheese smells awful but tastes surprisingly palatable on starchy crackers, unlike that insipid one you bought.
译文：这块奶酪气味刺鼻、闻着很冲，可配上淀粉多的饼干吃起来却意外地可口，不像你买的那块那么寡淡。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0656 -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###insipid food### 寡淡的食物

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0709 -->
# 雅思单词 0709 · 停止；中断
中文：v. 停止；中断；不连续
单词：###discontinue###
搭配：###discontinue a product### 停产某产品
例句：Some governments ###discontinue### notes of high denomination, an unobtrusive measure that circumscribes tax evasion and has become a keystone of anti-corruption policy.
译文：一些国家停止发行大面额纸币，这项不显眼的措施限制了逃税，已成为反腐政策的基石。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1202 -->

搭配：###notes of small denomination### 小面额钞票
例句：Some governments discontinue notes of high ###denomination###, an unobtrusive measure that circumscribes tax evasion and has become a keystone of anti-corruption policy.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0667 -->
# 雅思单词 0667 · 独奏会，独唱会；背诵

搭配：###a piano recital### 钢琴独奏会
例句：The ###recital### was phenomenal: the percussion was thrilling, and the soloist got a standing ovation!
译文：这场独奏音乐会太精彩了：打击乐激动人心，独奏者还赢得了全场起立鼓掌！

搭配：###phenomenal success### 巨大的成功

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0670 -->

搭配：###a standing ovation### 起立鼓掌

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0476 -->
# 雅思单词 0476 · 满载的
中文：adj. 装满的，载满的
单词：###laden###
搭配：###laden with### 满载着
例句：Ferries ###laden### with migrants, treated as outcasts at home, can capsize in rough seas, and plaintive calls for help mark this tragic exodus.
译文：载满移民的渡船可能在风浪中倾覆，这些人在家乡被当作弃儿，哀切的求救声成了这场悲惨大逃亡的标志。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0950 -->

搭配：###a social outcast### 被社会遗弃的人
例句：Ferries laden with migrants, treated as ###outcasts### at home, can capsize in rough seas, and plaintive calls for help mark this tragic exodus.
译文：载满移民的渡船可能在风浪中倾覆，这些人在家乡被当作弃儿，哀切的求救声成了这场悲惨大逃亡的标志。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0895 -->

搭配：###the prologue to the novel### 小说的序言
例句：The ###prologue### is thought to have been written under a pseudonym by a prolific poet whose identity remains elusive.

单词：###prolific###
搭配：###a prolific writer### 多产作家
例句：The prologue is thought to have been written under a pseudonym by a ###prolific### poet whose identity remains elusive.
译文：人们认为这篇序言是一位多产的诗人用笔名写的，而这位诗人的身份至今难以确定。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0291 · 裂缝

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0718 -->
# 雅思单词 0718 · 搅拌（牛奶制黄油）；翻腾
中文：v. 搅拌（牛奶制黄油）；翻腾；（因紧张而）反胃 n. 搅乳器
单词：###churn###
搭配：###churn butter### 搅制黄油
例句：Don't look at my bread with such disdain—look how glossy the crust is! I improvised the recipe and ###churned### the butter myself.
译文：别用那么嫌弃的眼神看我的面包——你看面包皮多有光泽！我是即兴配的方子，黄油也是自己搅的。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1103 -->
# 雅思单词 1103 · 标语牌
中文：n. 招贴，布告；标语牌
单词：###placard###
搭配：###wave placards### 挥舞标语牌
例句：Protesters waved ###placards### amid the commotion, condemning how police mishandled the dispersal of the crowd, yet refused to be goaded into violence.
译文：抗议者在一片混乱中举着标语牌，谴责警方驱散人群时处置不当，但拒绝被激怒而诉诸暴力。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1087 -->
# 雅思单词 1087 · 监督，主管
中文：v. 监督，指挥(工作)；主管
单词：###superintend###
搭配：###superintend the work### 监督工作
例句：A veteran engineer will ###superintend### construction, checking that every wall is plumb and that the cloakroom meets fire codes, so the hall gains accreditation.
译文：一位资深工程师将监督施工，检查每面墙是否垂直、衣帽间是否符合消防规定，好让大厅通过认证。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0735 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0347 -->
# 雅思单词 0347 · 助研助教职位

单词：###assistantship###
搭配：###a teaching assistantship### 助教职位
例句：Postgraduates often fund study through an ###assistantship###, working as adjunct tutors and helping with transcription, work that can conduce to their research.
译文：研究生常靠助研助教岗位支付学费，当兼职辅导老师、帮忙做文字转录，这些工作也有助于他们自己的研究。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1180 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0751 -->
# 雅思单词 0751 · 突然转向
中文：v. 突然转向
单词：###swerve###
搭配：###swerve to avoid### 急转避开
例句：I ###swerved### to avoid a squirrel, misjudged the curve and cracked my windscreen on a branch—what a mischance!
译文：我为了躲一只松鼠猛打方向盘，没判断好弯道，挡风玻璃被树枝砸裂了——真倒霉！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0752 -->

搭配：###a cracked windscreen### 裂了的挡风玻璃

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0755 -->
# 雅思单词 0755 · 盛会，庆典；选美比赛
中文：n. 露天表演，庆典；盛会；选美比赛
单词：###pageant###
搭配：###a historical pageant### 历史剧表演
例句：Critics call such summits a ###pageant### whose ostensible aim is cooperation but which merely legitimizes regimes that ignore inviolable rights.
译文：批评者称这类峰会只是一场盛大表演，表面上是为了合作，实际上却为那些无视不可侵犯的权利的政权披上合法外衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0756 -->

搭配：###the ostensible reason### 表面理由
例句：Critics call such summits a pageant whose ###ostensible### aim is cooperation but which merely legitimizes regimes that ignore inviolable rights.
译文：批评者称这类峰会只是一场盛大表演，表面上是为了合作，实际上却为那些无视不可侵犯的权利的政权披上合法外衣。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0757 -->

搭配：###legitimize a regime### 使政权合法化

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0758 -->

搭配：###inviolable rights### 神圣不可侵犯的权利

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0763 -->
# 雅思单词 0763 · 压缩，束紧；限制

单词：###constrict###
搭配：###constrict blood vessels### 使血管收缩
例句：These boots ###constrict### my toes so badly that I'm slouching and walking in an ungainly way—luckily they're refundable!
译文：这双靴子把我的脚趾挤得太难受了，害得我弓着背、走路很难看——还好可以退款！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0159 -->
# 雅思单词 0159 · 百年纪念；百年的

单词：###centennial###

例句：During the town's ###centennial###, volunteers placed water dispensers along the route, fountains gushed, and from noon onwards prodigious crowds gathered.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0779 -->
# 雅思单词 0779 · 无数的；无数
中文：adj. 无数的 n. 无数，极大数量
单词：###myriad###
搭配：###a myriad of### 无数的
例句：A reef hosts ###myriad### species, from plankton to sharks, making it a microcosm of ocean life and a living laboratory for microbiology.
译文：一片礁石上生活着无数物种，从浮游生物到鲨鱼应有尽有，这让它成为海洋生物的缩影，也是微生物学的天然实验室。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0780 -->
# 雅思单词 0780 · 浮游生物

单词：###plankton###
搭配：###marine plankton### 海洋浮游生物
例句：A reef hosts myriad species, from ###plankton### to sharks, making it a microcosm of ocean life and a living laboratory for microbiology.
译文：一片礁石上生活着无数物种，从浮游生物到鲨鱼应有尽有，这让它成为海洋生物的缩影，也是微生物学的天然实验室。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0781 -->

搭配：###a school motto### 校训
例句：Whatever ###motto### a firm adopts, it soon becomes a platitude unless leaders reiterate its values and remain steadfast.
译文：无论一家公司采用什么口号，如果领导者不反复重申其价值观并坚定不移，它很快就会变成一句空话。

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0785 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0787 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0992 -->
# 雅思单词 0992 · 属于；有关
中文：v. 附属；有关
单词：###pertain###
搭配：###pertain to### 与……有关
例句：Advice ###pertaining### to creative work suggests that writers who seclude themselves need unconditional family support and the will to fend off distractions.
译文：关于创作的建议认为，闭门写作的作家需要家人无条件的支持，以及抵挡干扰的意志。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1096 -->

搭配：###offshore wind farms### 海上风电场
例句：When ###offshore### fisheries are overexploited, stocks collapse, and coastal villages lose their main source of sustenance and nutritional security.
译文：近海渔场一旦遭到过度捕捞，鱼类资源就会崩溃，沿海村庄也会失去主要的食物来源和营养保障。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0831 -->
# 雅思单词 0831 · 充满（问题、危险等）的；担忧的

单词：###fraught###
搭配：###fraught with danger### 充满危险
例句：###Fraught### with tension, the talks dragged on until an ultimatum forced both sides to accept a truce and reciprocate gestures of goodwill.
译文：谈判充满紧张气氛，拖了很久，直到一份最后通牒迫使双方接受停火，并互相释放善意。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0832 -->

例句：Fraught with tension, the talks dragged on until an ###ultimatum### forced both sides to accept a truce and reciprocate gestures of goodwill.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0844 -->
# 雅思单词 0844 · 手术刀

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0845 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0848 -->

译文：我们的花园计划终于实现了：多年生的玫瑰开出了大大的花瓣，常春藤也缠满了大门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0849 -->

# 雅思单词 0851 · 缠绕
中文：vt. 使缠住，使盘绕；缠绕
单词：###entwine###
搭配：###be entwined with### 与……交织在一起
例句：Our garden plan has come to fruition: the perennial roses have huge petals, and ivy has ###entwined### itself around the gate!
译文：我们的花园计划终于实现了：多年生的玫瑰开出了大大的花瓣，常春藤也缠满了大门！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###scamper away### 一溜烟跑开
例句：Grab the sledge and follow the dog ###scampering### up the hill, but stay off the ice by the waterfront—it's starting to thaw!
译文：拿上雪橇，跟着那条往山上蹿的狗走，不过别去湖边的冰面——冰已经开始化了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0947 -->

单词：###waterfront###
搭配：###a waterfront restaurant### 滨水餐厅
例句：Grab the sledge and follow the dog scampering up the hill, but stay off the ice by the ###waterfront###—it's starting to thaw!
译文：拿上雪橇，跟着那条往山上蹿的狗走，不过别去湖边的冰面——冰已经开始化了！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0948 -->

译文：拿上雪橇，跟着那条往山上蹿的狗走，不过别去湖边的冰面——冰已经开始化了！

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0957 -->
# 雅思单词 0957 · 未察觉的，忘却的
中文：adj. 不在意的，忘却的；未察觉的
单词：###oblivious###
搭配：###be oblivious to### 对……浑然不觉
例句：My cat is ###oblivious### to the fireworks; she's curled up on a ball of yarn, unconcerned, while the dog wails under the bed.
译文：我的猫对烟花声毫无察觉，蜷在一团毛线上，一副事不关己的样子，而狗却在床底下哀嚎。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0958 -->

搭配：###spin a yarn### 编故事
例句：My cat is oblivious to the fireworks; she's curled up on a ball of ###yarn###, unconcerned, while the dog wails under the bed.
译文：我的猫对烟花声毫无察觉，蜷在一团毛线上，一副事不关己的样子，而狗却在床底下哀嚎。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

搭配：###be unconcerned about### 对……不在意

译文：我的猫对烟花声毫无察觉，蜷在一团毛线上，一副事不关己的样子，而狗却在床底下哀嚎。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：My cat is oblivious to the fireworks; she's curled up on a ball of yarn, unconcerned, while the dog ###wails### under the bed.

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0961 -->
# 雅思单词 0961 · 商业的，贸易的
中文：adj. 商业的；贸易的
单词：###mercantile###
搭配：###a mercantile class### 商人阶层
例句：###Mercantile### cities on navigable rivers intermixed goods and languages, and the interplay of cultures there reshaped art and religion.
译文：坐落在可通航河流上的商业城市汇聚了各地的货物和语言，那里的文化交融重塑了艺术和宗教。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0962 -->

搭配：###a navigable river### 可通航的河流
例句：Mercantile cities on ###navigable### rivers intermixed goods and languages, and the interplay of cultures there reshaped art and religion.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Mercantile cities on navigable rivers ###intermixed### goods and languages, and the interplay of cultures there reshaped art and religion.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0500 -->

搭配：###a summer intern### 暑期实习生

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w0445 -->
# 雅思单词 0445 · 牧民

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1001 -->

例句：Hardly had the ###overture###, a prelude to the opera, begun when a phone rang, drawing a vehement glare and laughter from onlookers.
译文：作为歌剧序幕的序曲刚刚开始，就有手机响了，引来愤怒的瞪视和旁观者的笑声。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1005 -->

单词：###wean###
搭配：###wean sb. off### 使某人戒掉
例句：Our puppy, ###weaned### only last month, swallowed a sock and is having spasms—call the vet before he regurgitates it!
译文：我们的小狗上个月才断奶，吞下了一只袜子，现在一阵阵抽搐——趁它还没吐出来，快叫兽医！
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1006 -->

搭配：###muscle spasms### 肌肉痉挛

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1013 -->

译文：这部长达九百页的中世纪论著已由一丝不苟的志愿者转录完毕，并将在网上发布。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1017 -->

搭配：###run on a treadmill### 在跑步机上跑步
例句：After a week on the ###treadmill### building my physique, I made a wager with Dad that I could sprint to the corner in ten seconds.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1018 -->

译文：在跑步机上练了一周体格之后，我跟爸爸打赌，说我十秒钟就能冲到街角。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1020 -->
# 雅思单词 1020 · 冲刺，短跑；短跑
中文：v. 全速奔跑，冲刺 n. 短跑；冲刺
单词：###sprint###
搭配：###sprint for the bus### 冲去赶公交
例句：After a week on the treadmill building my physique, I made a wager with Dad that I could ###sprint### to the corner in ten seconds.
译文：在跑步机上练了一周体格之后，我跟爸爸打赌，说我十秒钟就能冲到街角。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1021 -->
# 雅思单词 1021 · 取代，代替
中文：v. 代替；取代
单词：###supersede###
搭配：###be superseded by### 被……取代
例句：Electric cars may soon ###supersede### petrol models as sales outstrip forecasts, so governments underwrite battery research, the pivot of the industry's future.
译文：随着销量超出预期，电动汽车可能很快取代燃油车，所以各国政府为电池研究提供资金保障，因为它是汽车业未来的关键。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1022 -->

例句：Electric cars may soon supersede petrol models as sales ###outstrip### forecasts, so governments underwrite battery research, the pivot of the industry's future.
译文：随着销量超出预期，电动汽车可能很快取代燃油车，所以各国政府为电池研究提供资金保障，因为它是汽车业未来的关键。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1025 -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1050 -->
# 雅思单词 1050 · 不屈的；坚硬的

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1053 -->

搭配：###wooden slats### 木条
例句：Careful! That bench has missing ###slats###, loose rivets and a sharp notch, and the wet paint will smear your jeans.

<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1057 -->
# 雅思单词 1057 · 贮存，囤积；储备

单词：###stockpile###
搭配：###stockpile weapons### 囤积武器
例句：Nations ###stockpile### grain and fuel for emergencies, and in wartime they may requisition private stashes, a prerogative few challenge.
译文：各国会储备粮食和燃料以备紧急情况，战时还可能征用私人囤积的物资，这是很少有人质疑的特权。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1065 -->

单词：###numerate###
搭配：###literate and numerate### 能读会算
例句：I'm not very ###numerate###—is this discount really twofold, or is the sign skewed to fool us? Put it on my tab anyway.
译文：我算数不太好——这个折扣真的翻倍了吗，还是标牌故意误导我们？不管了，先记在我账上吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1066 -->

例句：I'm not very numerate—is this discount really ###twofold###, or is the sign skewed to fool us? Put it on my tab anyway.

单词：###tab###
搭配：###pick up the tab### 付账
例句：I'm not very numerate—is this discount really twofold, or is the sign skewed to fool us? Put it on my ###tab### anyway.
译文：我算数不太好——这个折扣真的翻倍了吗，还是标牌故意误导我们？不管了，先记在我账上吧。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1089 -->
# 雅思单词 1089 · 透支（额）

搭配：###run up an overdraft### 透支
例句：My ###overdraft###'s used up and I'm eking out scant savings—could you lend me cash until my parents' remittance arrives?

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1097 -->
# 雅思单词 1097 · 替罪羊
中文：n. 替罪羊
单词：###scapegoat###
搭配：###make sb. a scapegoat### 让某人当替罪羊
例句：It was a junior clerk who became the ###scapegoat###, a mere semblance of justice hiding the board's ulterior motives and undue influence.
译文：被当作替罪羊的是一名初级职员，这不过是表面上的公正，掩盖了董事会别有用心的动机和不正当的影响。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1121 -->
# 雅思单词 1121 · 遣返，把……送回国

单词：###repatriate###
搭配：###repatriate prisoners### 遣返战俘
例句：Were the government to ###repatriate### the refugees now, it would transgress international law, and victims of such an inhumane act would have no recourse.
译文：如果政府现在就把难民遣返回国，就会违反国际法，而这种不人道行为的受害者将求助无门。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 0827 · 再开发，改造
中文：n. 重新规划；重新建设；再开发；改造
单词：###redevelopment###
搭配：###urban redevelopment### 城市改造
例句：The council pledged rapid ###redevelopment### of the docks on the city's periphery and vowed to replenish green space, a promise that residents met with disenchantment.
译文：市政会承诺迅速改造城市边缘的码头，还发誓要补充绿地，可居民们对这个承诺并不抱希望。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- /leiji-note -->

例句：Stop being so ###neurotic### about germs! Health takes precedence, but this mania for hand gel is cracking your skin, and scolding guests isn't politic.

# 雅思单词 1250 · 输血；灌输

单词：###transfuse###
搭配：###transfuse blood### 输血
例句：In the final trimester, complications may require doctors to ###transfuse### blood or give saline drips to protect the baby in the womb.
译文：在孕晚期，如果出现并发症，医生可能需要输血或输生理盐水，以保护子宫里的胎儿。

# 雅思单词 1265 · 有感知力的

单词：###sentient###
搭配：###sentient beings### 有知觉的生物
例句：Scientists now deem octopuses ###sentient###, and genes in their DNA helix include enhancers controlling ink secretion and brain growth.
译文：科学家现在认为章鱼具有感知能力，它们 DNA 双螺旋中的基因包含控制喷墨分泌和大脑发育的增强子。

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1273 -->
# 雅思单词 1273 · 简洁的，简明的
中文：adj. 简明的，扼要的；简洁的
单词：###succinct###
搭配：###a succinct summary### 简明的总结
例句：Career changers should write a ###succinct### application that highlights skills rather than perquisites, wiping the slate clean to gain traction with recruiters.
译文：转行的人应该写一份简明扼要的申请，突出技能而非以往的福利待遇，一切从头开始，才更容易打动招聘人员。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

<!-- leiji-note-id:danci-ielts-words-w1277 -->
# 雅思单词 1277 · 推进剂

单词：###propellant###
搭配：###rocket propellant### 火箭推进剂
例句：Formed when gas acts as a ###propellant### during the extrusion of lava, pumice is so light that it floats, and its layers overlie older rock.
译文：浮石是熔岩喷出时气体起推动作用而形成的，它轻得能浮在水面上，其岩层覆盖在较古老的岩石之上。
<!-- /leiji-note -->

# 雅思单词 1281 · 缺点；（学校）记过，扣分

搭配：###merits and demerits### 优缺点
例句：Don't let one ###demerit### fetter your ambition—it's an uphill climb, but you can recapture top marks next term!
译文：别让一次扣分束缚住你的志向——虽然要费点劲，但下学期你一定能重新拿回第一！

# 雅思单词 1285 · 编码，把……译成密码

单词：###encode###
搭配：###encode data### 给数据编码
例句：After the hospital ###encoded### its natal records digitally, the paper files were consigned to storage and declared null and void.
译文：医院把出生记录数字化之后，纸质档案就被送进库房封存，并宣布作废。
<!-- /leiji-note -->
